# Upstream provenance and local edition

Source: https://github.com/kaomei/stickman-video-director/tree/10287822ba47579321068da1b93801face9160dd/skills/directing-stickman-videos
Immutable source commit: `10287822ba47579321068da1b93801face9160dd`. Local packaging version: `2026.11.24` (not an upstream release).

Copyright of the upstream skill, fonts, audio-library evidence and LICENSE remains with the upstream authors and the credited third parties. The MIT LICENSE, nested audio licenses and font OFL texts are preserved verbatim.

## Changes and exclusions

- Vendored a slim market-local optional package from skills/directing-stickman-videos plus the repository-root MIT LICENSE, so the installer contentDir contains the license text.
- Kept scripts, fonts, licenses, catalogs, style boards and approved stills that this snapshot can ship under the ordinary catalog size budget.
- Moved B-audio binaries, B6 style-lock reference videos, the B6 paint kit and approved B8 reference/replay packs to an explicit fetch manifest (pin-fixed raw.githubusercontent.com URLs + SHA-256). The fetch script is not run at install and does not execute downloads.
- Excluded README demos, skill examples, public showcases, B5 local baselines and B6 Hong Kong WIP example productions.
- Catalog slug is stickman-video-director; upstream frontmatter name directing-stickman-videos is preserved in references/upstream-skill.md.
